## the first paint on the main screen
world: plain, a terminal that names itself to nothing: no atomic drawing, no extended keys, no pointer shapes
> a tree of two lines is given
out \e[?25l
  modes: dec[] cursor=hidden kitty=0 otherkeys=0 region=whole pointer=unset
  held: cursor-hidden
  ink: alt=no mouse=yes prefers=yes wheel=no entry=no reasserts=0 exit=none
  stdin: raw=off readable=0
  pools: the 1. set

  timers: 100
  selection: anchor=none focus=none dragging=no span=none band=whole alt-press=no off=0/0 has=no
  screen: empty
> 99 ms pass
  timers: 1
> 1 ms pass
out \e[1Gone\r\ntwo\r\n
  frame 1: reads=13 renderer=1 diff=3 optimize=1 write=2 layout=1 commit=1 patches=7 nodes=11/2/6/4
  timers: none

    |one
    |two
> the same tree is given again
  timers: 16
> 16 ms pass
  frame 2: reads=12 renderer=1 diff=3 optimize=1 write=1 layout=1 commit=1 patches=0 nodes=1/0/1/4
  timers: none
> 40 ms pass
> a third line is given after the frame window has passed
out \e[1Gthree\r\n
  frame 3: reads=13 renderer=1 diff=3 optimize=1 write=2 layout=1 commit=1 patches=4 nodes=15/1/11/5
  screen:
    |one
    |two
    |three
> the screen is unmounted
  frame 4: reads=12 renderer=1 diff=3 optimize=1 write=1 layout=0 commit=0 patches=0 nodes=15/1/11/5
